{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QxjXMnbicaaif6eVe8dMFt","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env python3\n2\t\"\"\"Evaluate contrastive direction vectors on held-out test pairs.\n3\t\n4\tInput:\n5\t  --directions  directions.pt from compute_directions.py\n6\t  --activations activations.pt from extract_activations.py\n7\t  --output-dir  directory for JSON report\n8\t\n9\tOutput:\n10\t  evaluation_report.json — per-layer AUROC, cosine similarity, stratified\n11\t                           breakdown, Go/No-Go verdict\n12\t  Human-readable summary printed to stdout\n13\t\n14\tDeps: torch, numpy, sklearn\n15\t\"\"\"\n16\t\n17\timport argparse\n18\timport json\n19\timport logging\n20\timport sys\n21\tfrom collections import defaultdict\n22\tfrom pathlib import Path\n23\t\n24\timport numpy as np\n25\timport torch\n26\tfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n27\t\n28\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef load_test_samples(\n32\t    activations_path: str, test_pair_ids: list[str]\n33\t) -> list[dict]:\n34\t    \"\"\"Load activation records and keep only those in the test set.\"\"\"\n35\t    records = torch.load(activations_path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n36\t    test_set = set(test_pair_ids)\n37\t    filtered = [r for r in records if r[\"pair_id\"] in test_set]\n38\t    logger.info(\n39\t        \"Loaded %d total records; %d belong to test set (%d test pair_ids)\",\n40\t        len(records), len(filtered), len(test_set),\n41\t    )\n42\t    return filtered\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef compute_auroc_per_layer(\n46\t    samples: list[dict], d_syc: torch.Tensor\n47\t) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]:\n48\t    \"\"\"Compute AUROC per layer and token position.\n49\t\n50\t    For each sample, project its activation onto d_syc (dot product) to get a\n51\t    scalar score. Label: 1=invalid_pressure, 0=valid_correction.\n52\t\n53\t    Returns {layer_idx: {token_pos: auroc}}.\n54\t    \"\"\"\n55\t    num_layers = d_syc.shape[0]\n56\t    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\n57\t\n58\t    labels = []\n59\t    # scores[layer][pos] -> list of floats\n60\t    scores: dict[int, dict[int, list]] = {\n61\t        l: {p: [] for p in range(num_positions)} for l in range(num_layers)\n62\t    }\n63\t\n64\t    for sample in samples:\n65\t        label = 1 if sample[\"condition\"] == \"invalid_pressure\" else 0\n66\t        labels.append(label)\n67\t        acts = sample[\"activations\"]  # (num_layers, 2, hidden_dim)\n68\t\n69\t        for layer in range(num_layers):\n70\t            for pos in range(num_positions):\n71\t                score = torch.dot(acts[layer, pos], d_syc[layer, pos]).item()\n72\t                scores[layer][pos].append(score)\n73\t\n74\t    labels_arr = np.array(labels)\n75\t    results = {}\n76\t    for layer in range(num_layers):\n77\t        results[layer] = {}\n78\t        for pos in range(num_positions):\n79\t            scores_arr = np.array(scores[layer][pos])\n80\t            if len(np.unique(labels_arr)) < 2:\n81\t                results[layer][pos] = float(\"nan\")\n82\t            else:\n83\t                results[layer][pos] = roc_auc_score(labels_arr, scores_arr)\n84\t\n85\t    return results\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tdef compute_cosine_per_layer(\n89\t    d_syc: torch.Tensor, d_gen: torch.Tensor\n90\t) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]:\n91\t    \"\"\"Compute cosine similarity between d_syc and d_gen per layer and token position.\"\"\"\n92\t    num_layers = d_syc.shape[0]\n93\t    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\n94\t\n95\t    results = {}\n96\t    for layer in range(num_layers):\n97\t        results[layer] = {}\n98\t        for pos in range(num_positions):\n99\t            cos = torch.nn.functional.cosine_similarity(\n100\t                d_syc[layer, pos].unsqueeze(0),\n101\t                d_gen[layer, pos].unsqueeze(0),\n102\t            ).item()\n103\t            results[layer][pos] = cos\n104\t\n105\t    return results\n106\t\n107\t\n108\tdef compute_stratified_auroc(\n109\t    samples: list[dict], d_syc: torch.Tensor, group_key: str, best_layer: int\n110\t) -> dict[str, dict[int, float]]:\n111\t    \"\"\"Compute AUROC broken down by a metadata field (domain, evidence_strength, etc.).\n112\t\n113\t    Uses the best layer only, across all token positions.\n114\t    \"\"\"\n115\t    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\n116\t\n117\t    groups: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)\n118\t    for s in samples:\n119\t        groups[str(s[group_key])].append(s)\n120\t\n121\t    results = {}\n122\t    for group_val, group_samples in sorted(groups.items()):\n123\t        labels = []\n124\t        scores_by_pos: dict[int, list] = {p: [] for p in range(num_positions)}\n125\t\n126\t        for sample in group_samples:\n127\t            label = 1 if sample[\"condition\"] == \"invalid_pressure\" else 0\n128\t            labels.append(label)\n129\t            for pos in range(num_positions):\n130\t                score = torch.dot(\n131\t                    sample[\"activations\"][best_layer, pos], d_syc[best_layer, pos]\n132\t                ).item()\n133\t                scores_by_pos[pos].append(score)\n134\t\n135\t        labels_arr = np.array(labels)\n136\t        if len(np.unique(labels_arr)) < 2:\n137\t            results[group_val] = {p: float(\"nan\") for p in range(num_positions)}\n138\t        else:\n139\t            results[group_val] = {\n140\t                p: roc_auc_score(labels_arr, np.array(scores_by_pos[p]))\n141\t                for p in range(num_positions)\n142\t            }\n143\t\n144\t    return results\n145\t\n146\t\n147\tdef go_nogo_verdict(\n148\t    best_auroc: float, best_cosine: float,\n149\t    layer0_auroc: float = 0.5, layer0_delta: float = 1.0,\n150\t) -> dict:\n151\t    \"\"\"Determine Go/No-Go based on v2 thresholds.\n152\t\n153\t    Criteria (all must hold for PASS):\n154\t      1. Best-layer AUROC >= 0.85\n155\t      2. Δ(best_layer - layer0) >= 0.15 (signal in model computation, not embedding)\n156\t      3. cosine(d_syc, d_gen) < 0.3\n157\t\n158\t    GRAY: AUROC in [0.80, 0.85) or Δ in [0.10, 0.15)\n159\t    FAIL: python3\n\"\"\"Evaluate contrastive direction vectors on held-out test pairs.\n\nInput:\n  --directions  directions.pt from compute_directions.py\n  --activations activations.pt from extract_activations.py\n  --output-dir  directory for JSON report\n\nOutput:\n  evaluation_report.json — per-layer AUROC, cosine similarity, stratified\n                           breakdown, Go/No-Go verdict\n  Human-readable summary printed to stdout\n\nDeps: torch, numpy, sklearn\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport sys\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef load_test_samples(\n    activations_path: str, test_pair_ids: list[str]\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Load activation records and keep only those in the test set.\"\"\"\n    records = torch.load(activations_path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n    test_set = set(test_pair_ids)\n    filtered = [r for r in records if r[\"pair_id\"] in test_set]\n    logger.info(\n        \"Loaded %d total records; %d belong to test set (%d test pair_ids)\",\n        len(records), len(filtered), len(test_set),\n    )\n    return filtered\n\n\ndef compute_auroc_per_layer(\n    samples: list[dict], d_syc: torch.Tensor\n) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Compute AUROC per layer and token position.\n\n    For each sample, project its activation onto d_syc (dot product) to get a\n    scalar score. Label: 1=invalid_pressure, 0=valid_correction.\n\n    Returns {layer_idx: {token_pos: auroc}}.\n    \"\"\"\n    num_layers = d_syc.shape[0]\n    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\n\n    labels = []\n    # scores[layer][pos] -> list of floats\n    scores: dict[int, dict[int, list]] = {\n        l: {p: [] for p in range(num_positions)} for l in range(num_layers)\n    }\n\n    for sample in samples:\n        label = 1 if sample[\"condition\"] == \"invalid_pressure\" else 0\n        labels.append(label)\n        acts = sample[\"activations\"]  # (num_layers, 2, hidden_dim)\n\n        for layer in range(num_layers):\n            for pos in range(num_positions):\n                score = torch.dot(acts[layer, pos], d_syc[layer, pos]).item()\n                scores[layer][pos].append(score)\n\n    labels_arr = np.array(labels)\n    results = {}\n    for layer in range(num_layers):\n        results[layer] = {}\n        for pos in range(num_positions):\n            scores_arr = np.array(scores[layer][pos])\n            if len(np.unique(labels_arr)) < 2:\n                results[layer][pos] = float(\"nan\")\n            else:\n                results[layer][pos] = roc_auc_score(labels_arr, scores_arr)\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_cosine_per_layer(\n    d_syc: torch.Tensor, d_gen: torch.Tensor\n) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Compute cosine similarity between d_syc and d_gen per layer and token position.\"\"\"\n    num_layers = d_syc.shape[0]\n    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\n\n    results = {}\n    for layer in range(num_layers):\n        results[layer] = {}\n        for pos in range(num_positions):\n            cos = torch.nn.functional.cosine_similarity(\n                d_syc[layer, pos].unsqueeze(0),\n                d_gen[layer, pos].unsqueeze(0),\n            ).item()\n            results[layer][pos] = cos\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef compute_stratified_auroc(\n    samples: list[dict], d_syc: torch.Tensor, group_key: str, best_layer: int\n) -> dict[str, dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Compute AUROC broken down by a metadata field (domain, evidence_strength, etc.).\n\n    Uses the best layer only, across all token positions.\n    \"\"\"\n    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\n\n    groups: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)\n    for s in samples:\n        groups[str(s[group_key])].append(s)\n\n    results = {}\n    for group_val, group_samples in sorted(groups.items()):\n        labels = []\n        scores_by_pos: dict[int, list] = {p: [] for p in range(num_positions)}\n\n        for sample in group_samples:\n            label = 1 if sample[\"condition\"] == \"invalid_pressure\" else 0\n            labels.append(label)\n            for pos in range(num_positions):\n                score = torch.dot(\n                    sample[\"activations\"][best_layer, pos], d_syc[best_layer, pos]\n                ).item()\n                scores_by_pos[pos].append(score)\n\n        labels_arr = np.array(labels)\n        if len(np.unique(labels_arr)) < 2:\n            results[group_val] = {p: float(\"nan\") for p in range(num_positions)}\n        else:\n            results[group_val] = {\n                p: roc_auc_score(labels_arr, np.array(scores_by_pos[p]))\n                for p in range(num_positions)\n            }\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef go_nogo_verdict(\n    best_auroc: float, best_cosine: float,\n    layer0_auroc: float = 0.5, layer0_delta: float = 1.0,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Determine Go/No-Go based on v2 thresholds.\n\n    Criteria (all must hold for PASS):\n      1. Best-layer AUROC >= 0.85\n      2. Δ(best_layer - layer0) >= 0.15 (signal in model computation, not embedding)\n      3. cosine(d_syc, d_gen) < 0.3\n\n    GRAY: AUROC in [0.80, 0.85) or Δ in [0.10, 0.15)\n    FAIL: AUROC < 0.80 or Δ < 0.10\n    \"\"\"\n    cosine_ok = best_cosine < 0.3\n    delta_ok = layer0_delta >= 0.15\n    delta_gray = layer0_delta >= 0.10\n\n    reasons = []\n    if best_auroc >= 0.85 and delta_ok and cosine_ok:\n        verdict = \"PASS\"\n        reasons.append(f\"AUROC={best_auroc:.4f} >= 0.85\")\n        reasons.append(f\"Δ(best-layer0)={layer0_delta:.4f} >= 0.15\")\n        reasons.append(f\"cosine={best_cosine:.4f} < 0.3\")\n    elif best_auroc < 0.80 or layer0_delta < 0.10:\n        verdict = \"FAIL\"\n        if best_auroc < 0.80:\n            reasons.append(f\"AUROC={best_auroc:.4f} < 0.80\")\n        if layer0_delta < 0.10:\n            reasons.append(f\"Δ(best-layer0)={layer0_delta:.4f} < 0.10 (confound risk)\")\n    else:\n        verdict = \"GRAY\"\n        if best_auroc < 0.85:\n            reasons.append(f\"AUROC={best_auroc:.4f} in [0.80, 0.85)\")\n        if not delta_ok:\n            reasons.append(f\"Δ(best-layer0)={layer0_delta:.4f} in [0.10, 0.15)\")\n        if not cosine_ok:\n            reasons.append(f\"cosine={best_cosine:.4f} >= 0.3\")\n\n    return {\n        \"verdict\": verdict,\n        \"reason\": \"; \".join(reasons),\n        \"auroc\": best_auroc,\n        \"cosine\": best_cosine,\n        \"layer0_auroc\": layer0_auroc,\n        \"layer0_delta\": layer0_delta,\n    }\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Evaluate contrastive direction vectors on test set.\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--directions\", required=True, help=\"Path to directions.pt from compute_directions.py\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--activations\", required=True, help=\"Path to activations.pt from extract_activations.py\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output-dir\", default=\".\", help=\"Output directory (default: .)\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n\n    logger.info(\"Loading directions from %s\", args.directions)\n    directions = torch.load(args.directions, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n    d_syc = directions[\"d_syc\"]\n    d_gen = directions[\"d_gen\"]\n    test_pair_ids = directions[\"test_pair_ids\"]\n    metadata = directions[\"metadata\"]\n\n    if not test_pair_ids:\n        logger.error(\"No test pair IDs in directions file; exiting\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    test_samples = load_test_samples(args.activations, test_pair_ids)\n    if not test_samples:\n        logger.error(\"No test samples found; exiting\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    # Per-layer AUROC\n    logger.info(\"Computing per-layer AUROC\")\n    auroc_per_layer = compute_auroc_per_layer(test_samples, d_syc)\n\n    # Find best layer (max AUROC across token positions)\n    best_layer = -1\n    best_auroc = -1.0\n    best_pos = -1\n    for layer, pos_dict in auroc_per_layer.items():\n        for pos, auc in pos_dict.items():\n            if not np.isnan(auc) and auc > best_auroc:\n                best_auroc = auc\n                best_layer = layer\n                best_pos = pos\n\n    logger.info(\"Best layer=%d, pos=%d, AUROC=%.4f\", best_layer, best_pos, best_auroc)\n\n    # Cosine similarity\n    logger.info(\"Computing per-layer cosine similarity\")\n    cosine_per_layer = compute_cosine_per_layer(d_syc, d_gen)\n    best_cosine = cosine_per_layer[best_layer][best_pos]\n\n    # Stratified analysis at best layer\n    logger.info(\"Computing stratified AUROC breakdown\")\n    breakdown_domain = compute_stratified_auroc(test_samples, d_syc, \"domain\", best_layer)\n    breakdown_evidence = compute_stratified_auroc(\n        test_samples, d_syc, \"evidence_strength\", best_layer\n    )\n    # Layer 0 confound baseline: if signal is already strong at layer 0,\n    # it may be a surface-level token artifact rather than a learned representation.\n    layer0_auroc = max(auroc_per_layer[0].values()) if 0 in auroc_per_layer else 0.5\n    layer0_delta = best_auroc - layer0_auroc\n\n    # Go/No-Go\n    verdict = go_nogo_verdict(best_auroc, best_cosine, layer0_auroc, layer0_delta)\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"metadata\": metadata,\n        \"num_test_samples\": len(test_samples),\n        \"num_test_pairs\": len(test_pair_ids),\n        \"best_layer\": best_layer,\n        \"best_token_position\": best_pos,\n        \"best_auroc\": best_auroc,\n        \"best_cosine\": best_cosine,\n        \"layer0_auroc\": layer0_auroc,\n        \"layer0_delta\": layer0_delta,\n        \"per_layer_auroc\": {\n            str(l): {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\n            for l, pos_dict in auroc_per_layer.items()\n        },\n        \"per_layer_cosine\": {\n            str(l): {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\n            for l, pos_dict in cosine_per_layer.items()\n        },\n        \"stratified_auroc\": {\n            \"by_domain\": {\n                k: {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\n                for k, pos_dict in breakdown_domain.items()\n            },\n            \"by_evidence_strength\": {\n                k: {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\n                for k, pos_dict in breakdown_evidence.items()\n            },\n        },\n        \"go_nogo\": verdict,\n        \"note\": (\n            \"In the current binary setup d_gen = -d_syc, so cosine(d_syc, d_gen) = -1.0. Path(args.output_dir)\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    report_path = out_dir / \"evaluation_report.json\"\n    with open(report_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n    logger.info(\"Saved report to %s\", report_path)\n\n    # Print human-readable summary\n    token_pos_names = {0: \"user_last\", 1: \"asst_first\"}\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"DIRECTION EVALUATION SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Test pairs: {len(test_pair_ids)}  |  Test samples: {len(test_samples)}\")\n    print(f\"Layers: {metadata['num_layers']}  |  Hidden dim: {metadata['hidden_dim']}\")\n    print()\n\n    print(f\"Best layer: {best_layer}  |  Token position: {best_pos} ({token_pos_names.get(best_pos, '?')})\")\n    print(f\"Best AUROC: {best_auroc:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Layer 0 AUROC: {layer0_auroc:.4f}  |  Delta(best - layer0): {layer0_delta:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Cosine(d_syc, d_gen) at best: {best_cosine:.4f}  (expected: -1.0 in binary setup)\")\n    print()\n\n    # Top 5 layers\n    all_layer_auc = []\n    for layer, pos_dict in auroc_per_layer.items():\n        for pos, auc in pos_dict.items():\n            if not np.isnan(auc):\n                all_layer_auc.append((layer, pos, auc))\n    all_layer_auc.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\n\n    print(\"Top 5 (layer, position, AUROC):\")\n    for layer, pos, auc in all_layer_auc[:5]:\n        print(f\"  Layer {layer:2d}, {token_pos_names.get(pos, f'pos{pos}'):10s}: {auc:.4f}\")\n    print()\n\n    # Stratified breakdown\n    print(\"AUROC by domain (best layer, all positions):\")\n    for domain, pos_dict in sorted(breakdown_domain.items()):\n        vals = [f\"{token_pos_names.get(p, f'pos{p}')}={v:.4f}\" for p, v in sorted(pos_dict.items())]\n        print(f\"  {domain:25s}  {', '.join(vals)}\")\n    print()\n\n    print(\"AUROC by evidence_strength (best layer, all positions):\")\n    for es, pos_dict in sorted(breakdown_evidence.items()):\n        vals = [f\"{token_pos_names.get(p, f'pos{p}')}={v:.4f}\" for p, v in sorted(pos_dict.items())]\n        print(f\"  {es:25s}  {', '.join(vals)}\")\n    print()\n\n    # Go/No-Go\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n    print(f\"GO/NO-GO VERDICT: {verdict['verdict']}\")\n    print(f\"  {verdict['reason']}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","numLines":361,"startLine":1,"totalLines":361}}}

stdout\\n\\nDeps: torch, numpy, sklearn\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\ndef load_test_samples(\\n    activations_path: str, test_pair_ids: list[str]\\n) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load activation records and keep only those in the test set.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    records = torch.load(activations_path, map_location=\\\"cpu\\\", weights_only=False)\\n    test_set = set(test_pair_ids)\\n    filtered = [r for r in records if r[\\\"pair_id\\\"] in test_set]\\n    logger.info(\\n        \\\"Loaded %d total records; %d belong to test set (%d test pair_ids)\\\",\\n        len(records), len(filtered), len(test_set),\\n    )\\n    return filtered\\n\\n\\ndef compute_auroc_per_layer(\\n    samples: list[dict], d_syc: torch.Tensor\\n) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute AUROC per layer and token position.\\n\\n    For each sample, project its activation onto d_syc (dot product) to get a\\n    scalar score. Label: 1=invalid_pressure, 0=valid_correction.\\n\\n    Returns {layer_idx: {token_pos: auroc}}.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    num_layers = d_syc.shape[0]\\n    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\\n\\n    labels = []\\n    # scores[layer][pos] -> list of floats\\n    scores: dict[int, dict[int, list]] = {\\n        l: {p: [] for p in range(num_positions)} for l in range(num_layers)\\n    }\\n\\n    for sample in samples:\\n        label = 1 if sample[\\\"condition\\\"] == \\\"invalid_pressure\\\" else 0\\n        labels.append(label)\\n        acts = sample[\\\"activations\\\"]  # (num_layers, 2, hidden_dim)\\n\\n        for layer in range(num_layers):\\n            for pos in range(num_positions):\\n                score = torch.dot(acts[layer, pos], d_syc[layer, pos]).item()\\n                scores[layer][pos].append(score)\\n\\n    labels_arr = np.array(labels)\\n    results = {}\\n    for layer in range(num_layers):\\n        results[layer] = {}\\n        for pos in range(num_positions):\\n            scores_arr = np.array(scores[layer][pos])\\n            if len(np.unique(labels_arr)) < 2:\\n                results[layer][pos] = float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n            else:\\n                results[layer][pos] = roc_auc_score(labels_arr, scores_arr)\\n\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef compute_cosine_per_layer(\\n    d_syc: torch.Tensor, d_gen: torch.Tensor\\n) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute cosine similarity between d_syc and d_gen per layer and token position.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    num_layers = d_syc.shape[0]\\n    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\\n\\n    results = {}\\n    for layer in range(num_layers):\\n        results[layer] = {}\\n        for pos in range(num_positions):\\n            cos = torch.nn.functional.cosine_similarity(\\n                d_syc[layer, pos].unsqueeze(0),\\n                d_gen[layer, pos].unsqueeze(0),\\n            ).item()\\n            results[layer][pos] = cos\\n\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef compute_stratified_auroc(\\n    samples: list[dict], d_syc: torch.Tensor, group_key: str, best_layer: int\\n) -> dict[str, dict[int, float]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute AUROC broken down by a metadata field (domain, evidence_strength, etc.).\\n\\n    Uses the best layer only, across all token positions.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    num_positions = d_syc.shape[1]\\n\\n    groups: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)\\n    for s in samples:\\n        groups[str(s[group_key])].append(s)\\n\\n    results = {}\\n    for group_val, group_samples in sorted(groups.items()):\\n        labels = []\\n        scores_by_pos: dict[int, list] = {p: [] for p in range(num_positions)}\\n\\n        for sample in group_samples:\\n            label = 1 if sample[\\\"condition\\\"] == \\\"\\n\\n... Go/No-Go\\n    verdict = go_nogo_verdict(best_auroc, best_cosine, layer0_auroc, layer0_delta)\\n\\n    # Build report\\n    report = {\\n        \\\"metadata\\\": metadata,\\n        \\\"num_test_samples\\\": len(test_samples),\\n        \\\"num_test_pairs\\\": len(test_pair_ids),\\n        \\\"best_layer\\\": best_layer,\\n        \\\"best_token_position\\\": best_pos,\\n        \\\"best_auroc\\\": best_auroc,\\n        \\\"best_cosine\\\": best_cosine,\\n        \\\"layer0_auroc\\\": layer0_auroc,\\n        \\\"layer0_delta\\\": layer0_delta,\\n        \\\"per_layer_auroc\\\": {\\n            str(l): {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\\n            for l, pos_dict in auroc_per_layer.items()\\n        },\\n        \\\"per_layer_cosine\\\": {\\n            str(l): {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\\n            for l, pos_dict in cosine_per_layer.items()\\n        },\\n        \\\"stratified_auroc\\\": {\\n            \\\"by_domain\\\": {\\n                k: {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\\n                for k, pos_dict in breakdown_domain.items()\\n            },\\n            \\\"by_evidence_strength\\\": {\\n                k: {str(p): v for p, v in pos_dict.items()}\\n                for k, pos_dict in breakdown_evidence.items()\\n            },\\n        },\\n        \\\"go_nogo\\\": verdict,\\n        \\\"note\\\": (\\n            \\\"In the current binary setup d_gen = -d_syc, so cosine(d_syc, d_gen) = -1.0. \\\"\\n            \\\"The cosine threshold check (< 0.3) becomes meaningful only when d_gen is \\\"\\n            \\\"independently defined.\\\"\\n        ),\\n    }\\n\\n    # Save JSON report\\n    out_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    report_path = out_dir / \\\"evaluation_report.json\\\"\\n    with open(report_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saved report to %s\\\", report_path)\\n\\n    # Print human-readable summary\\n    token_pos_names = {0: \\\"user_last\\\", 1: \\\"asst_first\\\"}\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(\\\"DIRECTION EVALUATION SUMMARY\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    print(f\\\"Test pairs: {len(test_pair_ids)}  |  Test samples: {len(test_samples)}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Layers: {metadata['num_layers']}  |  Hidden dim: {metadata['hidden_dim']}\\\")\\n    print()\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Best layer: {best_layer}  |  Token position: {best_pos} ({token_pos_names.get(best_pos, '?')})\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Best AUROC: {best_auroc:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Layer 0 AUROC: {layer0_auroc:.4f}  |  Delta(best - layer0): {layer0_delta:.4f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"Cosine(d_syc, d_gen) at best: {best_cosine:.4f}  (expected: -1.0 in binary setup)\\\")\\n    print()\\n\\n    # Top 5 layers\\n    all_layer_auc = []\\n    for layer, pos_dict in auroc_per_layer.items():\\n        for pos, auc in pos_dict.items():\\n            if not np.isnan(auc):\\n                all_layer_auc.append((layer, pos, auc))\\n    all_layer_auc.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)\\n\\n    print(\\\"Top 5 (layer, position, AUROC):\\\")\\n    for layer, pos, auc in all_layer_auc[:5]:\\n        print(f\\\"  Layer {layer:2d}, {token_pos_names.get(pos, f'pos{pos}'):10s}: {auc:.4f}\\\")\\n    print()\\n\\n    # Stratified breakdown\\n    print(\\\"AUROC by domain (best layer, all positions):\\\")\\n    for domain, pos_dict in sorted(breakdown_domain.items()):\\n        vals = [f\\\"{token_pos_names.get(p, f'pos{p}')}={v:.4f}\\\" for p, v in sorted(pos_dict.items())]\\n        print(f\\\"  {domain:25s}  {', '.join(vals)}\\\")\\n    print()\\n\\n    print(\\\"AUROC by evidence_strength (best layer, all positions):\\\")\\n    for es, pos_dict in sorted(breakdown_evidence.items()):\\n        vals = [f\\\"{token_pos_names.get(p, f'pos{p}')}={v:.4f}\\\" for p, v in sorted(pos_dict.items())]\\n        print(f\\\"  {es:25s}  {', '.join(vals)}\\\")\\n    print()\\n\\n    # Go/No-Go\\n    print(\\\"-\\\" * 60)\\n    print(f\\\"GO/NO-GO VERDICT: {verdict['verdict']}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  {verdict['reason']}\\\")\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"}}}

\\\"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Compute contrastive direction vectors (d_syc, d_gen) from paired activations.\\\\n\\\\nInput:  .pt file from extract_activations.py — list[dict] with keys:\\\\n        {pair_id, condition, domain, evidence_strength, correction_turn,\\\\n         activations: Tensor(num_layers, 2, hidden_dim)}\\\\n        Each pair_id has two entries: condition=\\\\\\\"warranted_revision\\\\\\\" and\\\\n        condition=\\\\\\\"sycophantic_capitulation\\\\\\\".\\\\n\\\\nOutput: directions.pt — dict with keys:\\\\n        d_syc              (num_layers, 2, hidden_dim) — L2-normalized\\\\n        d_gen              (num_layers, 2, hidden_dim) — L2-normalized\\\\n        train_pair_ids     list[str]\\\\n        test_pair_ids      list[str]\\\\n        metadata           dict\\\\n\\\\nDeps:   torch, numpy, sklearn (for StratifiedShuffleSplit)\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport sys\\\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedShuffleSplit\\\\n\\\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_and_group(path: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, dict]]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load activation records and group by pair_id.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns {pair_id: {\\\\\\\"warranted_revision\\\\\\\": record, \\\\\\\"sycophantic_capitulation\\\\\\\": record}}.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\", weights_only=False)\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Loaded %d records from %s\\\\\\\", len(records), path)\\\\n\\\\n    grouped: dict[str, dict[str, dict]] = defaultdict(dict)\\\\n    for rec in records:\\\\n        pid = rec[\\\\\\\"pair_id\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        cond = rec[\\\\\\\"condition\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        if cond in grouped[pid]:\\\\n            logger.warning(\\\\\\\"Duplicate condition '%s' for pair_id '%s'; overwriting\\\\\\\", cond, pid)\\\\n        grouped[pid][cond] = rec\\\\n\\\\n    complete = {}\\\\n    for pid, conds in grouped.items():\\\\n        if \\\\\\\"warranted_revision\\\\\\\" in conds and \\\\\\\"sycophantic_capitulation\\\\\\\" in conds:\\\\n            complete[pid] = conds\\\\n        else:\\\\n            logger.warning(\\\\n                \\\\\\\"Pair '%s' incomplete (conditions: %s); skipping\\\\\\\", pid, list(conds.keys())\\\\n            )\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Complete pairs: %d\\\\\\\", len(complete))\\\\n    return complete\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef stratified_split(\\\\n    pairs: dict[str, dict[str, dict]], test_ratio: float, seed: int\\\\n) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Split pair_ids into train/test with stratification by domain.\\\\n\\\\n    Uses StratifiedShuffleSplit so each domain's proportion is preserved\\\\n    across the split. The split unit is the pair (both conditions stay together).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\\\\n    domains = [pairs[pid][\\\\\\\"warranted_revision\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"domain\\\\\\\"] for pid in pair_ids]\\\\n\\\\n    # Check if stratification is feasible (need >= 2 samples per domain for split)\\\\n    domain_counts = defaultdict(int)\\\\n    for d in domains:\\\\n        domain_counts[d] += 1\\\\n\\\\n    min_count = min(domain_counts.values())\\\\n    if min_count < 2:\\\\n        logger.warning(\\\\n            \\\\\\\"Some domains have < 2 pairs (min=%d); falling back to unstratified shuffle\\\\\\\",\\\\n            min_count,\\\\n        )\\\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\\\n        idx = rng.permutation(len(pair_ids))\\\\n        n_test = max(1, int(len(pair_ids) * test_ratio))\\\\n        test_idx = set(idx[:n_test])\\\\n        train_ids = [pair_ids[i] for i in range(len(pair_ids)) if i not in test_idx]\\\\n        test_ids = [pair_ids[i] for i in range(len(pair_ids)) if i in test_idx]\\\\n        return train_ids, test_ids\\\\n\\\\n    splitter = StratifiedShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=test_ratio, random_state=seed)\\\\n    pair_ids_arr = np.array(pair_ids)\\\\n    domains_arr = np.array(domains)\\\\n\\\\n    train_idx, test_idx = next(splitter.split(pair_ids_arr, domains_arr))\\\\n    train_ids = pair_ids_arr[train_idx].tolist()\\\\n    test_ids = pair_ids_arr[test_idx].tolist()\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Split: %d train, %d test\\\\\\\", len(train_ids), len(test_ids))\\\\n    return train_ids, test_ids\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_direction(\\\\n    pairs: dict[str, dict[str, dict]],\\\\n    pair_ids: list[str],\\\\n    positive_condition: str,\\\\n    negative_condition: str,\\\\n) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Compute difference-in-means direction: mean(positive) - mean(negative).\\\\n\\\\n    Returns L2-normalized direction of shape (num_layers, 2, hidden_dim).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    pos_acts = []\\\\n    neg_acts = []\\\\n    for pid in pair_ids:\\\\n        pos_acts.append(pairs[pid][positive_condition][\\\\\\\"activations\\\\\\\"])\\\\n        neg_acts.append(pairs[pid][negative_condition][\\\\\\\"activations\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n    # Stack: (N, num_layers, 2, hidden_dim)\\\\n    pos_mean = torch.stack(pos_acts).mean(dim=0)\\\\n    neg_mean = torch.stack(neg_acts).mean(dim=0)\\\\n\\\\n    direction = pos_mean - neg_mean\\\\n\\\\n    # L2 normalize per (layer, token_position) slice\\\\n    norms = direction.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8)\\\\n    direction = direction / norms\\\\n\\\\n    return direction\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef main() -> None:\\\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\\\\n        description=\\\\\\\"Compute contrastive direction vectors from paired activations.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    )\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\", help=\\\\\\\"Path to activations .pt file (Phase 2 output)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--output-dir\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\".\\\\\\\", help=\\\\\\\"Output directory (default: .)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\n        \\\\\\\"--test-ratio\\\\\\\", type=float, default=0.2, help=\\\\\\\"Test set ratio (default: 0.2)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    )\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--seed\\\\\\\", type=int, default=42, help=\\\\\\\"Random seed (default: 42)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n    logging.basicConfig(\\\\n        level=logging.INFO,\\\\n        format=\\\\\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\\\\\\\",\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    pairs = load_and_group(args.input)\\\\n    if not pairs:\\\\n        logger.error(\\\\\\\"No complete pairs found; exiting\\\\\\\")\\\\n        sys.exit(1)\\\\n\\\\n    train_ids, test_ids = stratified_split(pairs, args.test_ratio, args.seed)\\\\n\\\\n    # d_syc: sycophantic_capitulation - warranted_revision (on train set)\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Computing d_syc from %d train pairs\\\\\\\", len(train_ids))\\\\n    d_syc = compute_direction(\\\\n        pairs, train_ids,\\\\n        positive_condition=\\\\\\\"sycophantic_capitulation\\\\\\\",\\\\n        negative_condition=\\\\\\\"warranted_revision\\\\\\\",\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    # d_gen: warranted_revision - sycophantic_capitulation (on train set)\\\\n    # Currently d_gen = -d_syc, but stored separately for future extensibility.\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Computing d_gen from %d train pairs\\\\\\\", len(train_ids))\\\\n    d_gen = compute_direction(\\\\n        pairs, train_ids,\\\\n        positive_condition=\\\\\\\"warranted_revision\\\\\\\",\\\\n        negative_condition=\\\\\\\"sycophantic_capitulation\\\\\\\",\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    num_layers, _, hidden_dim = d_syc.shape\\\\n    metadata = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"num_layers\\\\\\\": num_layers,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"hidden_dim\\\\\\\": hidden_dim,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"test_ratio\\\\\\\": args.test_ratio,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"seed\\\\\\\": args.seed,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"num_train_pairs\\\\\\\": len(train_ids),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"num_test_pairs\\\\\\\": len(test_ids),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"num_total_pairs\\\\\\\": len(pairs),\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n    out_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\\\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\\\n    out_path = out_dir / \\\\\\\"directions.pt\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    payload = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"d_syc\\\\\\\": d_syc,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"d_gen\\\\\\\": d_gen,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"train_pair_ids\\\\\\\": train_ids,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"test_pair_ids\\\\\\\": test_ids,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"metadata\\\\\\\": metadata,\\\\n    }\\\\n    torch.save(payload, out_path)\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Saved directions to %s\\\\\\\", out_path)\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Metadata: %s\\\\\\\", metadata)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nif __name__ == \\\\\\\"__main__\\\\\\\":\\\\n    main()\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": tuple[list[str], list[str]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Split pair_ids into train/test with stratification by domain.\\n\\n    Uses StratifiedShuffleSplit so each domain's proportion is preserved\\n    across the split. The split unit is the pair (both conditions stay together).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\\n    domains = [pairs[pid][\\\"warranted_revision\\\"][\\\"domain\\\"] for pid in pair_ids]\\n\\n    # Check if stratification is feasible (need >= 2 samples per domain for split)\\n    domain_counts = defaultdict(int)\\n    for d in domains:\\n        domain_counts[d] += 1\\n\\n    min_count = min(domain_counts.values())\\n    if min_count < 2:\\n        logger.warning(\\n            \\\"Some domains have < 2 pairs (min=%d); falling back to unstratified shuffle\\\",\\n            min_count,\\n        )\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n        idx = rng.permutation(len(pair_ids))\\n        n_test = max(1, int(len(pair_ids) * test_ratio))\\n        test_idx = set(idx[:n_test])\\n        train_ids = [pair_ids[i] for i in range(len(pair_ids)) if i not in test_idx]\\n        test_ids = [pair_ids[i] for i in range(len(pair_ids)) if i in test_idx]\\n        return train_ids, test_ids\\n\\n    splitter = StratifiedShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=test_ratio, random_state=seed)\\n    pair_ids_arr = np.array(pair_ids)\\n    domains_arr = np.array(domains)\\n\\n    train_idx, test_idx = next(splitter.split(pair_ids_arr, domains_arr))\\n    train_ids = pair_ids_arr[train_idx].tolist()\\n    test_ids = pair_ids_arr[test_idx].tolist()\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Split: %d train, %d test\\\", len(train_ids), len(test_ids))\\n    return train_ids, test_ids\\n\\n\\ndef compute_direction(\\n    pairs: dict[str, dict[str, dict]],\\n    pair_ids: list[str],\\n    positive_condition: str,\\n    negative_condition: str,\\n) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute difference-in-means direction: mean(positive) - mean(negative).\\n\\n    Returns L2-normalized direction of shape (num_layers, 2, hidden_dim).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pos_acts = []\\n    neg_acts = []\\n    for pid in pair_ids:\\n        pos_acts.append(pairs[pid][positive_condition][\\\"activations\\\"])\\n        neg_acts.append(pairs[pid][negative_condition][\\\"activations\\\"])\\n\\n    # Stack: (N, num_layers, 2, hidden_dim)\\n    pos_mean = torch.stack(pos_acts).mean(dim=0)\\n    neg_mean = torch.stack(neg_acts).mean(dim=0)\\n\\n    direction = pos_mean - neg_mean\\n\\n    # L2 normalize per (layer, token_position) slice\\n    norms = direction.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8)\\n    direction = direction / norms\\n\\n    return direction\\n\\n\\ndef main() -> None:\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\\n        description=\\\"Compute contrastive direction vectors from paired activations.\\\"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"input\\\", help=\\\"Path to activations .pt file (Phase 2 output)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output-dir\\\", default=\\\".\\\", help=\\\"Output directory (default: .)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        \\\"--test-ratio\\\", type=float, default=0.2, help=\\\"Test set ratio (default: 0.2)\\\"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42, help=\\\"Random seed (default: 42)\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    logging.basicConfig(\\n        level=logging.INFO,\\n        format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\\\",\\n    )\\n\\n    pairs = load_and_group(args.input)\\n    if not pairs:\\n        logger.error(\\\"No complete pairs found; exiting\\\")\\n        sys.exit(1)\\n\\n    train_ids, test_ids = stratified_split(pairs, args.test_ratio, args.seed)\\n\\n    # d_syc: sycophantic_capitulation - warranted_revision (on train set)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Computing d_syc from %d train pairs\\\", len(train_ids))\\n    d_syc = compute_direction(\\n        pairs, train_ids,\\n        positive_condition=\\\"sycophantic_capitulation\\\",\\n        negative_condition=\\\"warranted_revision\\\",\\n    )\\n\\n    # d_gen: warranted_revision - sycophantic_capitulation (on train set)\\n    # Currently d_gen = -d_syc, but stored separately for future extensibility.\\n    logger.info(\\\"Computing d_gen from %d train pairs\\\", len(train_ids))\\n    d_gen = compute_direction(\\n        pairs, train_ids,\\n        positive_condition=\\\"warranted_revision\\\",\\n        negative_condition=\\\"sycophantic_capitulation\\\",\\n    )\\n\\n    num_layers, _, hidden_dim = d_syc.shape\\n    metadata = {\\n        \\\"num_layers\\\": num_layers,\\n        \\\"hidden_dim\\\": hidden_dim,\\n        \\\"test_ratio\\\": args.test_ratio,\\n        \\\"seed\\\": args.seed,\\n        \\\"num_train_pairs\\\": len(train_ids),\\n        \\\"num_test_pairs\\\": len(test_ids),\\n        \\\"num_total_pairs\\\": len(pairs),\\n    }\\n\\n    out_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    out_path = out_dir / \\\"directions.pt\\\"\\n\\n    payload = {\\n        \\\"d_syc\\\": d_syc,\\n        \\\"d_gen\\\": d_gen,\\n        \\\"train_pair_ids\\\": train_ids,\\n        \\\"test_pair_ids\\\": test_ids,\\n        \\\"metadata\\\": metadata,\\n    }\\n    torch.save(payload, out_path)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saved directions to %s\\\", out_path)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Metadata: %s\\\", metadata)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01DmppCRx4utqrDBp9q9BXnt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have all the info:\n\n**Qwen (exp001):**\n- 362 samples, 181 pairs\n- activations shape: (36, 2, 4096) — 36 layers, 2 token positions, 4096 dim\n- Key name: 'activations'\n- Strengths: weak=150, medium=108, strong=104 (samples, so 75/54/52 pairs)\n- CV AUROC: 0.827 (5-fold, best layer per fold, position 1 = asst_first)\n\n\n- Llama uses a different format: 16 layers instead of 36, single position (possibly averaged), and singular 'activation' key, achieving 0.779 AUROC on the same strength distribution.\n\nThe key structural difference is that Qwen includes a position dimension while Llama collapses it. Looking at the cv_auroc.json results, it seems a cross-validation pipeline ran 5 folds on the Qwen data, computing d_syc from each training fold and evaluating on the test set, with different optimal layers emerging across folds (19, 19, 19, 32, 35) all at the assistant's first token position. The bootstrap section then used the full dataset with layer 19 at that same position.\n\nFor my own implementation, I'm considering a 10000-iteration bootstrap approach where I resample the 181 pairs with replacement, run 5-fold CV on each resample, and record the mean AUROC to build a confidence interval around the CV estimate of 0.826. The computational cost is substantial though—each 5-fold CV requires computing 5 separate directions and scoring 5 test sets, which multiplied by 10000 iterations means 50000 direction computations, each involving the mean of roughly 145 tensors.\n\nI'm weighing three strategies: the full bootstrap-of-CV approach which is the most rigorous but computationally expensive, a faster alternative where I run 5-fold CV once and save the out-of-fold predictions, then bootstrap resample those predictions to compute AUROC, or a third option using the best layer and position from the full dataset to compute a fixed direction and then bootstrap the resulting scores.\n\nApproach B seems like the right call—it gives me statistical rigor without the computational overhead. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01A7dyr6Mbj1CSFmCHPK1tK2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JZzEG3DTHrjm5Tmavw7Diq","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-3/projects/contrastive_entrain_calib/analysis/bootstrap_auroc_ci.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Bootstrap 95% CI for d_syc AUROC metrics.\n\nComputes bootstrap CIs for:\n1. Qwen3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC (style-controlled data, 181 pairs)\n2. Qwen3-8B per-strength AUROC (weak/medium/strong)\n3. Llama-3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC\n4. d_syc vs random baseline AUROC difference\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\n\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\nN_FOLDS = 5\nSEED = 42\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef load_pairs(path, act_key=\"activations\"):\n    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n    pairs = defaultdict(dict)\n    for s in records:\n        pairs[s[\"pair_id\"]][s[\"condition\"]] = s\n    return {\n        pid: conds\n        for pid, conds in pairs.items()\n        if \"warranted_revision\" in conds and \"sycophantic_capitulation\" in conds\n    }\n\n\ndef prepare_arrays(pairs, act_key=\"activations\", pos=None):\n    \"\"\"Pre-extract activations as numpy arrays for speed.\n\n    Returns:\n        syc: (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n        wr:  (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n        domains: list[str]\n        strengths: list[str]\n        pair_ids: list[str]\n    \"\"\"\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\n    syc_list, wr_list, domains, strengths = [], [], [], []\n\n    for pid in pair_ids:\n        s_act = pairs[pid][\"sycophantic_capitulation\"][act_key]\n        w_act = pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][act_key]\n        if pos is not None:\n            s_act = s_act[:, pos, :]\n            w_act = w_act[:, pos, :]\n        syc_list.append(s_act)\n        wr_list.append(w_act)\n        domains.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"domain\"])\n        strengths.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"evidence_strength\"])\n\n    syc = torch.stack(syc_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    wr = torch.stack(wr_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    return syc, wr, domains, strengths, pair_ids\n\n\n# ── Core CV + AUROC ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_folds=N_FOLDS, seed=SEED,\n             layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None, return_oof=False):\n    \"\"\"Run stratified 5-fold CV and return mean AUROC (and optionally OOF scores).\n\n    For each fold: compute d_syc from train, search best layer on test, record AUROC.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = syc.shape[1]\n\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    fold_aurocs = []\n    oof_scores = np.zeros(n_pairs)  # syc_score - wr_score per pair (for OOF)\n    oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr):\n        d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d = d / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n\n        # Vectorized scoring: (n_test, n_layers)\n        s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n        w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n\n        best_auroc = -1.0\n        best_layer = layer_lo\n        for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n            scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n            labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n            try:\n                auroc = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                if auroc > best_auroc:\n                    best_auroc = auroc\n                    best_layer = layer\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n\n        fold_aurocs.append(best_auroc)\n        oof_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_layer]\n        oof_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_layer]\n\n    mean_auroc = float(np.mean(fold_aurocs))\n\n    if return_oof:\n        return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs, oof_syc, oof_wr\n    return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs\n\n\n# ── Bootstrap routines ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\n                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap the 5-fold CV AUROC by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    aurocs = []\n\n    for b in range(n_bootstrap):\n        boot_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        boot_domains = domains_arr[boot_idx]\n\n        # Ensure at least 2 unique domain values for stratification\n        if len(np.unique(boot_domains)) < N_FOLDS:\n            boot_domains = np.arange(n_pairs) % N_FOLDS\n\n        try:\n            mean_auc, _ = cv_auroc(\n                syc[boot_idx], wr[boot_idx], boot_domains,\n                seed=b, layer_lo=layer_lo, layer_hi=layer_hi,\n            )\n            aurocs.append(mean_auc)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    return {\n        \"mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n    }\n\n\ndef bootstrap_oof_auroc(oof_syc, oof_wr, mask=None,\n                         n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap AUROC from OOF predictions by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n    if mask is not None:\n        oof_syc = oof_syc[mask]\n        oof_wr = oof_wr[mask]\n\n    n = len(oof_syc)\n    if n < 4:\n        return None\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    aurocs = []\n\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc[idx], oof_wr[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            aurocs.append(roc_auc_score(labels, scores))\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    # Point estimate from full data\n    full_scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc, oof_wr])\n    full_labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(n), np.zeros(n)])\n    point_auroc = float(roc_auc_score(full_labels, full_scores))\n\n    return {\n        \"point_auroc\": point_auroc,\n        \"bootstrap_mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n        \"n_pairs\": n,\n        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n    }\n\n\ndef bootstrap_auroc_difference(oof_syc_d, oof_wr_d, oof_syc_r, oof_wr_r,\n                                n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap CI for AUROC(d_syc) - AUROC(random).\"\"\"\n    n = len(oof_syc_d)\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    diffs = []\n\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores_d = np.concatenate([oof_syc_d[idx], oof_wr_d[idx]])\n        scores_r = np.concatenate([oof_syc_r[idx], oof_wr_r[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            a_d = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_d)\n            a_r = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_r)\n            diffs.append(a_d - a_r)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    diffs = np.array(diffs)\n    return {\n        \"mean_diff\": float(np.mean(diffs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 97.5)),\n        \"p_greater_zero\": float(np.mean(diffs > 0)),\n        \"n_valid\": len(diffs),\n    }\n\n\ndef random_direction_oof(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_random=200, seed=SEED,\n                          layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    \"\"\"Compute mean AUROC of random directions (matched procedure to d_syc).\"\"\"\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs, n_layers, hidden = syc.shape\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = n_layers\n\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    folds = list(skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr))\n\n    random_aurocs = []\n    best_oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_overall = -1.0\n\n    for r in range(n_random):\n        d_rand = rng.randn(n_layers, hidden).astype(np.float32)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d_rand, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d_rand = d_rand / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n\n        fold_aurocs = []\n        tmp_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n        tmp_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n\n        for train_idx, test_idx in folds:\n            s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n            w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n\n            best_a = -1.0\n            best_l = layer_lo\n            for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n                scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n                labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n                try:\n                    a = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                    if a > best_a:\n                        best_a = a\n                        best_l = layer\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n\n            fold_aurocs.append(best_a)\n            tmp_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_l]\n            tmp_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_l]\n\n        mean_a = np.mean(fold_aurocs)\n        random_aurocs.append(mean_a)\n        if mean_a > best_overall:\n            best_overall = mean_a\n            best_oof_syc = tmp_syc.copy()\n            best_oof_wr = tmp_wr.copy()\n\n    return {\n        \"mean_auroc\": float(np.mean(random_aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(random_aurocs)),\n        \"max_auroc\": float(np.max(random_aurocs)),\n        \"n_random\": n_random,\n    }, best_oof_syc, best_oof_wr\n\n\n# ── Main ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef main():\n    t0 = time.time()\n    results = {}\n\n    # ── Qwen3-8B (exp001, 181 pairs, style-controlled) ────────────\n    print(\"Loading Qwen3-8B data (181 pairs)...\")\n    qwen_pairs = load_pairs(\n        \"/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp001/activations.pt\",\n        act_key=\"activations\",\n    )\n    syc_q, wr_q, domains_q, strengths_q, pids_q = prepare_arrays(\n        qwen_pairs, act_key=\"activations\", pos=1,  # asst_first\n    )\n    domains_q_arr = np.array(domains_q)\n    strengths_q_arr =     \"n_pairs\": int(syc_q.shape[0]),\n    }\n\n    # Bootstrap CI via OOF predictions (fast)\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen OOF AUROC (n=10000)...\")\n    q_oof_ci = bootstrap_oof_auroc(q_oof_syc, results[\"qwen_per_strength\"][strength] = ci\n            print(f\"  {strength}: AUROC = {ci['point_auroc']:.4f} \"\n                  f\"[{ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}] \"\n                  f\"(n={ci['n_pairs']})\")\n\n    # Full bootstrap-of-CV (slower but more rigorous)\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen full CV (n=10000, this takes a while)...\")\n    q_full_ci = f\"[{q_full_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {q_full_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n    print(f\"  Elapsed: {time.time() - t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    # ── Llama-3-8B (exp012, 181 pairs) ────────────────────────────\n  for strength in [\"weak\", \"medium\", \"strong\"]:\n        mask = strengths_l_arr == strength\n        ci = bootstrap_oof_auroc(l_oof_syc, l_oof_wr, mask=mask, seed=SEED)\n        if ci:\n               f\"(n={ci['n_pairs']})\")\n\n    # Full CV bootstrap for Llama\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Llama full CV (n=10000)...\")\n    l_full_ci = bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc_l, wr_l, domains_l_arr, {l_full_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n\n    # ── d_syc vs random baseline (Qwen) ──────────────────────────\n    print(\"\\nComputing random baseline (200 random directions)...\")\n    rand_stats, diff_ci = bootstrap_auroc_difference(\n        q_oof_syc, q_oof_wr, rand_oof_syc, rand_oof_wr, seed=SEED,\n    )\n    results[\"dsyc_vs_random_qwen\"] = {\n        \"dsyc_auroc\": q_oof_ci[\"point_auroc\"],\n        \"random_auroc\": rand_stats[\"mean_auroc\"],\n        \"difference_ci\": diff_ci,\n    }\n    print(f\"  AUROC diff = {diff_ci['mean_diff']:.4f} \"\n          f\"[{diff_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {diff_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}], \"\n          f\"P(diff>0) = {diff_ci['p_greater_zero']:.4f}\")\n\n    # ── Save results ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n    out_dir = Path(\"/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/artifacts\")\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path = out_dir / \"bootstrap_auroc_ci.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n\n    # ── Human-readable summary ────────────────────────────────────\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"BOOTSTRAP 95% CI SUMMARY (n_bootstrap=10000)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    print(\"\\n┌─ Qwen3-8B (style-controlled, 181 pairs) ─────────────────────────┐\")\n    print(f\"│ 5-fold CV AUROC = {q_mean:.4f} (std={np.std(q_folds):.4f})\")\n    print(f\"│ OOF bootstrap:  {q_oof_ci['point_auroc']:.4f} \"\n          f\"[{q_oof_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {q_oof_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n    print(f\"│ Full CV bootstrap: {q_full_ci['mean']:.4f} \"\n          f\"[{q_full_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {q_full_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n    print(\"│\")\n    print(\"│ Per-strength (OOF):\")\n    for s in [\"weak\", \"medium\", \"strong\"]:\n        ci = results[\"qwen_per_strength\"].get(s)\n        if ci:\n            print(f\"│   {s:8s}: {ci['point_auroc']:.4f} \"\n                  f\"[{ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}] print(\"│ Per-strength (OOF):\")\n    for s in [\"weak\", \"medium\", \"strong\"]:\n        ci = results[\"llama_per_strength\"].get(s)\n        if ci:\n            print(f\"│   {s:8s}: print(\"└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n┌─ d_syc vs random baseline (Qwen) ───────────────────────────────┐\")\n    print(f\"│ d_syc AUROC:  {q_oof_ci['point_auroc']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"│ Random AUROC: {rand_stats['mean_auroc']:.4f} (mean of {rand_stats['n_random']} random dirs)\")\n    print(f\"│ Δ(d_syc - random) = Qwen3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC (style-controlled data, 181 pairs)\n2. Qwen3-8B per-strength AUROC (weak/medium/strong)\n3. Llama-3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC\n4. d_syc vs random baseline AUROC difference\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\n\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\nN_FOLDS = 5\nSEED = 42\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef load_pairs(path, act_key=\"activations\"):\n    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n    pairs = defaultdict(dict)\n    for s in records:\n        pairs[s[\"pair_id\"]][s[\"condition\"]] = s\n    return {\n        pid: conds\n        for pid, conds in pairs.items()\n        if \"warranted_revision\" in conds and \"sycophantic_capitulation\" in conds\n    }\n\n\ndef prepare_arrays(pairs, act_key=\"activations\", pos=None):\n    \"\"\"Pre-extract activations as numpy arrays for speed.\n\n    Returns:\n        syc: (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n        wr:  (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n        domains: list[str]\n        strengths: list[str]\n        pair_ids: list[str]\n    \"\"\"\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\n    syc_list, wr_list, domains, strengths = [], [], [], []\n\n    for pid in pair_ids:\n        s_act = pairs[pid][\"sycophantic_capitulation\"][act_key]\n        w_act = pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][act_key]\n        if pos is not None:\n            s_act = s_act[:, pos, :]\n            w_act = w_act[:, pos, :]\n        syc_list.append(s_act)\n        wr_list.append(w_act)\n        domains.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"domain\"])\n        strengths.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"evidence_strength\"])\n\n    syc = torch.stack(syc_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    wr = torch.stack(wr_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    return syc, wr, domains, strengths, pair_ids\n\n\n# ── Core CV + AUROC ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_folds=N_FOLDS, seed=SEED,\n             layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None, return_oof=False):\n    \"\"\"Run stratified 5-fold CV and return mean AUROC (and optionally OOF scores).\n\n    For each fold: compute d_syc from train, search best layer on test, record AUROC.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = syc.shape[1]\n\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    fold_aurocs = []\n    oof_scores = np.zeros(n_pairs)  # syc_score - wr_score per pair (for OOF)\n    oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr):\n        d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d = d / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n\n        # Vectorized scoring: (n_test, n_layers)\n        s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n        w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n\n        best_auroc = -1.0\n        best_layer = layer_lo\n        for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n            scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n            labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n            try:\n                auroc = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                if auroc > best_auroc:\n                    best_auroc = auroc\n                    best_layer = layer\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n\n        fold_aurocs.append(best_auroc)\n        oof_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_layer]\n        oof_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_layer]\n\n    mean_auroc = float(np.mean(fold_aurocs))\n\n    if return_oof:\n        return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs, oof_syc, oof_wr\n    return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs\n\n\n# ── Bootstrap routines ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\n                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap the 5-fold CV AUROC by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    aurocs = []\n\n    for b in range(n_bootstrap):\n        boot_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        boot_domains = domains_arr[boot_idx]\n\n        # Ensure at least 2 unique domain values for stratification\n        if len(np.unique(boot_domains)) < N_FOLDS:\n            boot_domains = np.arange(n_pairs) % N_FOLDS\n\n        try:\n            mean_auc, _ = cv_auroc(\n                syc[boot_idx], wr[boot_idx], boot_domains,\n                seed=b, layer_lo=layer_lo, layer_hi=layer_hi,\n            )\n            aurocs.append(mean_auc)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    return {\n        \"mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n    }\n\n\ndef bootstrap_oof_auroc(oof_syc, oof_wr, mask=None,\n                         n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap AUROC from OOF predictions by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n    if mask is not None:\n        oof_syc = oof_syc[mask]\n        oof_wr = oof_wr[mask]\n\n    n = len(oof_syc)\n    if n < 4:\n        return None\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    aurocs = []\n\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc[idx], oof_wr[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            aurocs.append(roc_auc_score(labels, scores))\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    # Point estimate from full data\n    full_scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc, oof_wr])\n    full_labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(n), np.zeros(n)])\n    point_auroc = float(roc_auc_score(full_labels, full_scores))\n\n    return {\n        \"point_auroc\": point_auroc,\n        \"bootstrap_mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n        \"n_pairs\": n,\n        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n    }\n\n\ndef bootstrap_auroc_difference(oof_syc_d, oof_wr_d, oof_syc_r, oof_wr_r,\n                                n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap CI for AUROC(d_syc) - AUROC(random).\"\"\"\n    n = len(oof_syc_d)\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    diffs = []\n\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores_d = np.concatenate([oof_syc_d[idx], oof_wr_d[idx]])\n        scores_r = np.concatenate([oof_syc_r[idx], oof_wr_r[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            a_d = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_d)\n            a_r = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_r)\n            diffs.append(a_d - a_r)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    diffs = np.array(diffs)\n    return {\n        \"mean_diff\": float(np.mean(diffs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 97.5)),\n        \"p_greater_zero\": float(np.mean(diffs > 0)),\n        \"n_valid\": len(diffs),\n    }\n\n\ndef random_direction_oof(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_random=200, seed=SEED,\n                          layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    \"\"\"Compute mean AUROC of random directions (matched procedure to d_syc).\"\"\"\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs, n_layers, hidden = syc.shape\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = n_layers\n\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    folds = list(skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr))\n\n    random_aurocs = []\n    best_oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_overall = -1.0\n\n    for r in range(n_random):\n        d_rand = rng.randn(n_layers, hidden).astype(np.float32)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d_rand, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d_rand = d_rand / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n\n        fold_aurocs = []\n        tmp_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n        tmp_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n\n        for train_idx, test_idx in folds:\n            s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n            w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n\n            best_a = -1.0\n            best_l = layer_lo\n            for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n                scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n                labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n                try:\n                    a = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                    if a > best_a:\n                        best_a = a\n                        best_l = layer\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n\n            fold_aurocs.append(best_a)\n            tmp_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_l]\n            tmp_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_l]\n\n        mean_a = np.mean(fold_aurocs)\n        random_aurocs.append(mean_a)\n        if mean_a > best_overall:\n            best_overall = mean_a\n            best_oof_syc = tmp_syc.copy()\n            best_oof_wr = tmp_wr.copy()\n\n    return {\n        \"mean_auroc\": float(np.mean(random_aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(random_aurocs)),\n        \"max_auroc\": float(np.max(random_aurocs)),\n        \"n_random\": n_random,\n    }, best_oof_syc, best_oof_wr\n\n\n# ── Main
