Qwen3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC (style-controlled data, 181 pairs)\n6\t2. Qwen3-8B per-strength AUROC (weak/medium/strong)\n7\t3. Llama-3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC\n8\t4. d_syc vs random baseline AUROC difference\n9\t\"\"\"\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport sys\n13\timport time\n14\tfrom collections import defaultdict\n15\tfrom pathlib import Path\n16\t\n17\timport numpy as np\n18\timport torch\n19\tfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n20\tfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\n21\t\n22\tN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\n23\tN_FOLDS = 5\n24\tSEED = 42\n25\t\n26\t\n27\t# ── Data loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n28\t\n29\tdef load_pairs(path, act_key=\"activations\"):\n30\t    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n31\t    pairs = defaultdict(dict)\n32\t    for s in records:\n33\t        pairs[s[\"pair_id\"]][s[\"condition\"]] = s\n34\t    return {\n35\t        pid: conds\n36\t        for pid, conds in pairs.items()\n37\t        if \"warranted_revision\" in conds and \"sycophantic_capitulation\" in conds\n38\t    }\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef prepare_arrays(pairs, act_key=\"activations\", pos=None):\n42\t    \"\"\"Pre-extract activations as numpy arrays for speed.\n43\t\n44\t    Returns:\n45\t        syc: (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n46\t        wr:  (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n47\t        domains: list[str]\n48\t        strengths: list[str]\n49\t        pair_ids: list[str]\n50\t    \"\"\"\n51\t    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\n52\t    syc_list, wr_list, domains, strengths = [], [], [], []\n53\t\n54\t    for pid in pair_ids:\n55\t        s_act = pairs[pid][\"sycophantic_capitulation\"][act_key]\n56\t        w_act = pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][act_key]\n57\t        if pos is not None:\n58\t            s_act = s_act[:, pos, :]\n59\t            w_act = w_act[:, pos, :]\n60\t        syc_list.append(s_act)\n61\t        wr_list.append(w_act)\n62\t        domains.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"domain\"])\n63\t        strengths.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"evidence_strength\"])\n64\t\n65\t    syc = torch.stack(syc_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n66\t    wr = torch.stack(wr_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n67\t    return syc, wr, domains, strengths, pair_ids\n68\t\n69\t\n70\t# ── Core CV + AUROC ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n71\t\n72\tdef cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_folds=N_FOLDS, seed=SEED,\n73\t             layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None, return_oof=False):\n74\t    \"\"\"Run stratified 5-fold CV and return mean AUROC (and optionally OOF scores).\n75\t\n76\t    For each fold: compute d_syc from train, search best layer on test, record AUROC.\n77\t    \"\"\"\n78\t    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n79\t    if layer_hi is None:\n80\t        layer_hi = syc.shape[1]\n81\t\n82\t    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n83\t    fold_aurocs = []\n84\t    oof_scores = np.zeros(n_pairs)  # syc_score - wr_score per pair (for OOF)\n85\t    oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n86\t    oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n87\t\n88\t    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr):\n89\t        d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0)\n90\t        norms = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n91\t        d = d / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n92\t\n93\t        # Vectorized scoring: (n_test, n_layers)\n94\t        s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n95\t        w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n96\t\n97\t        best_auroc = -1.0\n98\t        best_layer = layer_lo\n99\t        for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n100\t            scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n101\t            labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n102\t            try:\n103\t                auroc = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n104\t                if auroc > best_auroc:\n105\t                    best_auroc = auroc\n106\t                    best_layer = layer\n107\t            except ValueError:\n108\t                pass\n109\t\n110\t        fold_aurocs.append(best_auroc)\n111\t        oof_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_layer]\n112\t        oof_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_layer]\n113\t\n114\t    mean_auroc = float(np.mean(fold_aurocs))\n115\t\n116\t    if return_oof:\n117\t        return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs, oof_syc, oof_wr\n118\t    return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs\n119\t\n120\t\n121\t# ── Bootstrap routines ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n122\t\n123\tdef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\n124\t                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n125\t    \"\"\"Bootstrap the 5-fold CV AUROC by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n126\t    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n127\t    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n128\t    aurocs = []\n129\t\n130\t    for b in range(n_bootstrap):\n131\t        boot_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n132\t        boot_domains = domains_arr[boot_idx]\n133\t\n134\t        # Ensure at least 2 unique domain values for stratification\n135\t        if len(np.unique(boot_domains)) < N_FOLDS:\n136\t            boot_domains = np.arange(n_pairs) % N_FOLDS\n137\t\n138\t        try:\n139\t            mean_auc, _ = cv_auroc(\n140\t                syc[boot_idx], wr[boot_idx], boot_domains,\n141\t                seed=b, layer_lo=layer_lo, layer_hi=layer_hi,\n142\t            )\n143\t            aurocs.append(mean_auc)\n144\t        except Exception:\n145\t            pass\n146\t\n147\t    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n148\t    return {\n149\t        \"mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n150\t        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n151\t        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n152\t        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n153\t        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n154\t    }\n155\t\n156\t\n157\tdef bootstrap_oof_auroc(oof_syc, oof_wr, mask=None,\n158\t                         n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n159\t    \"\"\"Bootstrap AUROC from OOF predictions by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n160\t    if mask is not None:\n161\t        oof_syc = oof_syc[mask]\n162\t        oof_wr = oof_wr[mask]\n163\t\n164\t    n = len(oof_syc)\n165\t    if n < 4:\n166\t        return None\n167\t\n168\t    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n169\t    aurocs = []\n170\t\n171\t    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n172\t        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n173\t        scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc[idx], oof_wr[idx]])\n174\t        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n175\t        try:\n176\t            aurocs.append(roc_auc_score(labels, scores))\n177\t        except ValueError:\n178\t            pass\n179\t\n180\t    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n181\t    # Point estimate from full data\n182\t    full_scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc, oof_wr])\n183\t    full_labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(n), np.zeros(n)])\n184\t    point_auroc = float(roc_auc_score(full_labels, full_scores))\n185\t\n186\t    return {\n187\t        \"point_auroc\": point_auroc,\n188\t        \"bootstrap_mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n189\t        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n190\t        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n191\t        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n192\t        \"n_pairs\": n,\n193\t        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n194\t    }\n195\t\n196\t\n197\tdef bootstrap_auroc_difference(oof_syc_d, oof_wr_d, oof_syc_r, oof_wr_r,\n198\t                                n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n199\t    \"\"\"Bootstrap CI for AUROC(d_syc) - AUROC(random).\"\"\"\n200\t    n = len(oof_syc_d)\n201\t    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n202\t    diffs = []\n203\t\n204\t    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n205\t        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n206\t        scores_d = np.concatenate([oof_syc_d[idx], oof_wr_d[idx]])\n207\t        scores_r = np.concatenate([oof_syc_r[idx], oof_wr_r[idx]])\n208\t        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n209\t        try:\n210\t            a_d = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_d)\n211\t            a_r = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_r)\n212\t            diffs.append(a_d - a_r)\n213\t        except ValueError:\n214\t            pass\n215\t\n216\t    diffs = np.array(diffs)\n217\t    return {\n218\t        \"mean_diff\": float(np.mean(diffs)),\n219\t        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 2.5)),\n220\t        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 97.5)),\n221\t        \"p_greater_zero\": float(np.mean(diffs > 0)),\n222\t        \"n_valid\": len(diffs),\n223\t    }\n224\t\n225\t\n226\tdef random_direction_oof(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_random=200, seed=SEED,\n227\t                          layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n228\t    \"\"\"Compute mean AUROC of random directions (matched procedure to d_syc).\"\"\"\n229\t    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n230\t    n_pairs, n_layers, hidden = syc.shape\n231\t    if layer_hi is None:\n232\t        layer_hi = n_layers\n233\t\n234\t    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n235\t    folds = list(skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr))\n236\t\n237\t    random_aurocs = []\n238\t    best_oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n239\t    best_oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n240\t    best_overall = -1.0\n241\t\n242\t    for r in range(n_random):\n243\t        d_rand = rng.randn(n_layers, hidden).astype(np.float32)\n244\t        norms = np.linalg.norm(d_rand, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n245\t        d_rand = d_rand / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n246\t\n247\t        fold_aurocs = []\n248\t        tmp_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n249\t        tmp_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n250\t\n251\t        for train_idx, test_idx in folds:\n252\t            s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n253\t            w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n254\t\n255\t            best_a = -1.0\n256\t            best_l = layer_lo\n257\t            for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n258\t                scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n259\t                labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n260\t                try:\n261\t                    a = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n262\t                    if a > best_a:\n263\t                        best_a = a\n264\t                        best_l = layer\n265\t                except ValueError:\n266\t                    pass\n267\t\n268\t            fold_aurocs.append(best_a)\n269\t            tmp_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_l]\n270\t            tmp_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_l]\n271\t\n272\t        mean_a = np.mean(fold_aurocs)\n273\t        random_aurocs.append(mean_a)\n274\t        if mean_a > best_overall:\n275\t            best_overall = mean_a\n276\t            best_oof_syc = tmp_syc.copy()\n277\t            best_oof_wr = tmp_wr.copy()\n278\t\n279\t    return {\n280\t        \"mean_auroc\": float(np.mean(random_aurocs)),\n281\t        \"std\": float(np.std(random_aurocs)),\n282\t        \"max_auroc\": float(np.max(random_aurocs)),\n283\t        \"n_random\": {syc_q.shape[0]} pairs, {syc_q.shape[1]} layers, {syc_q.shape[2]} dim\")\n305\t\n306\t    # Point estimate: 5-fold CV\n307\t    print(\"Running Qwen 5-fold CV (point estimate)...\")\n308\t    q_mean, python3\n\"\"\"Bootstrap 95% CI for d_syc AUROC metrics.\n\nComputes bootstrap CIs for:\n1. Qwen3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC (style-controlled data, 181 pairs)\n2. Qwen3-8B per-strength AUROC (weak/medium/strong)\n3. Llama-3-8B 5-fold CV AUROC\n4. d_syc vs random baseline AUROC difference\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\n\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\nN_FOLDS = 5\nSEED = 42\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef load_pairs(path, act_key=\"activations\"):\n    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n    pairs = defaultdict(dict)\n    for s in records:\n        pairs[s[\"pair_id\"]][s[\"condition\"]] = s\n    return {\n        pid: conds\n        for pid, conds in pairs.items()\n        if \"warranted_revision\" in conds and \"sycophantic_capitulation\" in conds\n    }\n\n\ndef prepare_arrays(pairs, act_key=\"activations\", pos=None):\n    \"\"\"Pre-extract activations as numpy arrays for speed.\n\n    Returns:\n        syc: (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n        wr:  (n_pairs, n_layers, hidden_dim)\n        domains: list[str]\n        strengths: list[str]\n        pair_ids: list[str]\n    \"\"\"\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\n    syc_list, wr_list, domains, strengths = [], [], [], []\n\n    for pid in pair_ids:\n        s_act = pairs[pid][\"sycophantic_capitulation\"][act_key]\n        w_act = pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][act_key]\n        if pos is not None:\n            s_act = s_act[:, pos, :]\n            w_act = w_act[:, pos, :]\n        syc_list.append(s_act)\n        wr_list.append(w_act)\n        domains.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"domain\"])\n        strengths.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"evidence_strength\"])\n\n    syc = torch.stack(syc_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    wr = torch.stack(wr_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    return syc, wr, domains, strengths, pair_ids\n\n\n# ── Core CV + AUROC ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_folds=N_FOLDS, seed=SEED,\n             layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None, return_oof=False):\n    \"\"\"Run stratified 5-fold CV and return mean AUROC (and optionally OOF scores).\n\n    For each fold: compute d_syc from train, search best layer on test, record AUROC.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = syc.shape[1]\n\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    fold_aurocs = []\n    oof_scores = np.zeros(n_pairs)  # syc_score - wr_score per pair (for OOF)\n    oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr):\n        d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d = d / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n\n        # Vectorized scoring: (n_test, n_layers)\n        s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n        w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n\n        best_auroc = -1.0\n        best_layer = layer_lo\n        for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n            scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n            labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n            try:\n                auroc = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                if auroc > best_auroc:\n                    best_auroc = auroc\n                    best_layer = layer\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n\n        fold_aurocs.append(best_auroc)\n        oof_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_layer]\n        oof_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_layer]\n\n    mean_auroc = float(np.mean(fold_aurocs))\n\n    if return_oof:\n        return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs, oof_syc, oof_wr\n    return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs\n\n\n# ── Bootstrap routines ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\n                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap the 5-fold CV AUROC by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    aurocs = []\n\n    for b in range(n_bootstrap):\n        boot_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        boot_domains = domains_arr[boot_idx]\n\n        # Ensure at least 2 unique domain values for stratification\n        if len(np.unique(boot_domains)) < N_FOLDS:\n            boot_domains = np.arange(n_pairs) % N_FOLDS\n\n        try:\n            mean_auc, _ = cv_auroc(\n                syc[boot_idx], wr[boot_idx], boot_domains,\n                seed=b, layer_lo=layer_lo, layer_hi=layer_hi,\n            )\n            aurocs.append(mean_auc)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    return {\n        \"mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n    }\n\n\ndef bootstrap_oof_auroc(oof_syc, oof_wr, mask=None,\n                         n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap AUROC from OOF predictions by resampling pairs.\"\"\"\n    if mask is not None:\n        oof_syc = oof_syc[mask]\n        oof_wr = oof_wr[mask]\n\n    n = len(oof_syc)\n    if n < 4:\n        return None\n\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    aurocs = []\n\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc[idx], oof_wr[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            aurocs.append(roc_auc_score(labels, scores))\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    # Point estimate from full data\n    full_scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc, oof_wr])\n    full_labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(n), np.zeros(n)])\n    point_auroc = float(roc_auc_score(full_labels, full_scores))\n\n    return {\n        \"point_auroc\": point_auroc,\n        \"bootstrap_mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n        \"n_pairs\": n,\n        \"n_valid\": len(aurocs),\n    }\n\n\ndef bootstrap_auroc_difference(oof_syc_d, oof_wr_d, oof_syc_r, oof_wr_r,\n                                n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    \"\"\"Bootstrap CI for AUROC(d_syc) - AUROC(random).\"\"\"\n    n = len(oof_syc_d)\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    diffs = []\n\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores_d = np.concatenate([oof_syc_d[idx], oof_wr_d[idx]])\n        scores_r = np.concatenate([oof_syc_r[idx], oof_wr_r[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            a_d = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_d)\n            a_r = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_r)\n            diffs.append(a_d - a_r)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    diffs = np.array(diffs)\n    return {\n        \"mean_diff\": float(np.mean(diffs)),\n        \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 97.5)),\n        \"p_greater_zero\": float(np.mean(diffs > 0)),\n        \"n_valid\": len(diffs),\n    }\n\n\ndef random_direction_oof(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_random=200, seed=SEED,\n                          layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    \"\"\"Compute mean AUROC of random directions (matched procedure to d_syc).\"\"\"\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs, n_layers, hidden = syc.shape\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = n_layers\n\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    folds = list(skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr))\n\n    random_aurocs = []\n    best_oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_overall = -1.0\n\n    for r in range(n_random):\n        d_rand = rng.randn(n_layers, hidden).astype(np.float32)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d_rand, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d_rand = d_rand / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n\n        fold_aurocs = []\n        tmp_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n        tmp_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n\n        for train_idx, test_idx in folds:\n            s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n            w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n\n            best_a = -1.0\n            best_l = layer_lo\n            for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n                scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n                labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n                try:\n                    a = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                    if a > best_a:\n                        best_a = a\n                        best_l = layer\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n\n            fold_aurocs.append(best_a)\n            tmp_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_l]\n            tmp_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_l]\n\n        mean_a = np.mean(fold_aurocs)\n        random_aurocs.append(mean_a)\n        if mean_a > best_overall:\n            best_overall = mean_a\n            best_oof_syc = tmp_syc.copy()\n            best_oof_wr = tmp_wr.copy()\n\n    return {\n        \"mean_auroc\": float(np.mean(random_aurocs)),\n        \"std\": float(np.std(random_aurocs)),\n        \"max_auroc\": float(np.max(random_aurocs)),\n        \"n_random\": n_random,\n    }, best_oof_syc, best_oof_wr\n\n\n# ── Main ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef main():\n    t0 = time.time()\n    results = {}\n\n    # ── Qwen3-8B (exp001, 181 pairs, style-controlled) ────────────\n    print(\"Loading Qwen3-8B data (181 pairs)...\")\n    qwen_pairs = load_pairs(\n        \"/root/contrastive_entrain_calib/results/exp001/activations.pt\",\n        act_key=\"activations\",\n    )\n    syc_q, wr_q, domains_q, strengths_q, pids_q = prepare_arrays(\n        qwen_pairs, act_key=\"activations\", pos=1,  # asst_first\n    )\n    domains_q_arr = np.array(domains_q)\n    strengths_q_arr = np.array(strengths_q)\n    print(f\"  {syc_q.shape[0]} pairs, {syc_q.shape[1]} layers, {syc_q.shape[2]} dim\")\n\n    # Point estimate: 5-fold CV\n    print(\"Running Qwen 5-fold CV (point estimate)...\")\n    q_mean, q_folds, q_oof_syc, q_oof_wr = cv_auroc(\n        syc_q, wr_q, domains_q_arr, return_oof=True,\n    )\n    print(f\"  Mean CV AUROC = {q_mean:.4f}, folds = {[round(x, 4) for x in q_folds]}\")\n\n    results[\"qwen_cv\"] = {\n        \"point_estimate\": q_mean,\n        \"fold_aurocs\": [round(x, 6) for x in q_folds],\n        \"fold_std\": float(np.std(q_folds)),\n        \"n_pairs\": int(syc_q.shape[0]),\n    }\n\n    # Bootstrap CI via OOF predictions (fast)\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen OOF AUROC (n=10000)...\")\n    q_oof_ci = bootstrap_oof_auroc(q_oof_syc, q_oof_wr, seed=SEED)\n    results[\"qwen_cv\"][\"oof_bootstrap\"] = q_oof_ci\n    print(f\"  OOF AUROC = {q_oof_ci['point_auroc']:.4f} \"\n          f\"[{q_oof_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {q_oof_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n\n    # Per-strength breakdown\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen per-strength AUROC...\")\n    results[\"qwen_per_strength\"] = {}\n    for strength in [\"weak\", \"medium\", \"strong\"]:\n        mask = strengths_q_arr == strength\n        ci = bootstrap_oof_auroc(q_oof_syc, q_oof_wr, mask=mask, seed=SEED)\n        if ci:\n            results[\"qwen_per_strength\"][strength] = ci\n            print(f\"  {strength}: AUROC = {ci['point_auroc']:.4f} \"\n                  f\"[{ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}] \"\n                  f\"(n={ci['n_pairs']})\")\n\n    # Full bootstrap-of-CV (slower but more rigorous)\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen full CV (n=10000, this takes a while)...\")\n    q_full_ci = bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc_q, wr_q, domains_q_arr, seed=SEED)\n    results[\"qwen_cv\"][\"full_cv_bootstrap\"] = q_full_ci\n    print(f\"  Full CV bootstrap: {q_full_ci['mean']:.4f} \"\n          f\"[{q_full_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {q_full_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n    print(f\"  Elapsed: {time.time() - t0:.1f}s\")\n\n    # ── Llama-3-8B (exp012, 181 pairs) ────────────────────────────\n act_key=\"activation\",\n    )\n    syc_l, wr_l, domains_l, strengths_l, pids_l = prepare_arrays(\n        llama_pairs, act_key=\"activation\", pos=None,  # no position dim\n    )\n    domains_l_arr = np.array(domains_l)\n    strengths_l_arr = np.array(strengths_l)\n    print(f\"  {syc_l.shape[0]} pairs, {syc_l.shape[1]} layers, {syc_l.shape[2]} dim\")\n\n    # Point estimate\n    print(\"Running Llama 5-fold CV...\")\n    l_mean, l_folds, l_oof_syc, l_oof_wr = cv_auroc(\n        syc_l, wr_l, domains_l_arr, return_oof=True,\n    )\n    print(f\"  Mean CV AUROC = {l_mean:.4f}, folds = f\"[{l_oof_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {l_oof_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n\n    # Llama per-strength\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Llama per-strength AUROC...\")\n    results[\"llama_per_strength\"] = {}\n    for strength in [\"weak\", \"medium\", \"strong\"]:\n        mask = strengths_l_arr == strength\n        ci = bootstrap_oof_auroc(l_oof_syc, l_oof_wr, mask=mask, seed=SEED)\n        if ci:\n            results[\"llama_per_strength\"][strength] = ci\n            print(f\"  {strength}: AUROC = {ci['point_auroc']:.4f} \"\n                  f\"[{ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}] \"\n {l_full_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n\n    # ── d_syc vs random baseline (Qwen) ──────────────────────────\n    print(\"\\nComputing random baseline (200 random directions)...\")\n    rand_stats, > /root/contrastive_entrain_calib/analysis/bootstrap_auroc_ci.py << 'SCRIPTEOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Bootstrap 95% CI for d_syc AUROC metrics.\"\"\"\nimport json, sys, time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\n\nN_BOOTSTRAP = 10000\nN_FOLDS = 5\nSEED = 42\n\ndef load_pairs(path, act_key=\"activations\"):\n    records = torch.load(path, map_location=\"cpu\", weights_only=False)\n    pairs = defaultdict(dict)\n    for s in records:\n        pairs[s[\"pair_id\"]][s[\"condition\"]] = s\n    return {pid: c for pid, c in pairs.items()\n            if \"warranted_revision\" in c and \"sycophantic_capitulation\" in c}\n\ndef prepare_arrays(pairs, act_key=\"activations\", pos=None):\n    pair_ids = sorted(pairs.keys())\n    syc_list, wr_list, domains, strengths = [], [], [], []\n    for pid in pair_ids:\n        s_act = pairs[pid][\"sycophantic_capitulation\"][act_key]\n        w_act = pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][act_key]\n        if pos is not None:\n            s_act = s_act[:, pos, :]\n            w_act = w_act[:, pos, :]\n        syc_list.append(s_act)\n        wr_list.append(w_act)\n        domains.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"domain\"])\n        strengths.append(pairs[pid][\"warranted_revision\"][\"evidence_strength\"])\n    syc = torch.stack(syc_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    wr = torch.stack(wr_list).numpy().astype(np.float32)\n    return syc, wr, domains, strengths, pair_ids\n\ndef cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_folds=N_FOLDS, seed=SEED,\n             layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None, return_oof=False):\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = syc.shape[1]\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    fold_aurocs = []\n    oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr):\n        d = syc[train_idx].mean(axis=0) - wr[train_idx].mean(axis=0)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d = d / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n        s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n        w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d).sum(axis=-1)\n        best_auroc = -1.0\n        best_layer = layer_lo\n        for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n            scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n            labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n            try:\n                auroc = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                if auroc > best_auroc:\n                    best_auroc = auroc\n                    best_layer = layer\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n        fold_aurocs.append(best_auroc)\n        oof_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_layer]\n        oof_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_layer]\n    mean_auroc = float(np.mean(fold_aurocs))\n    if return_oof:\n        return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs, oof_syc, oof_wr\n    return mean_auroc, fold_aurocs\n\ndef bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,\n                       seed=SEED, layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs = syc.shape[0]\n    aurocs = []\n    for b in range(n_bootstrap):\n        boot_idx = rng.choice(n_pairs, size=n_pairs, replace=True)\n        boot_domains = domains_arr[boot_idx]\n        if len(np.unique(boot_domains)) < N_FOLDS:\n            boot_domains = np.arange(n_pairs) % N_FOLDS\n        try:\n            mean_auc, _ = cv_auroc(\n                syc[boot_idx], wr[boot_idx], boot_domains,\n                seed=b, layer_lo=layer_lo, layer_hi=layer_hi)\n            aurocs.append(mean_auc)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        if (b+1) % 1000 == 0:\n            print(f\"  bootstrap {b+1}/{n_bootstrap}...\", flush=True)\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    return {\"mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)), \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n            \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n            \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n            \"n_valid\": len(aurocs)}\n\ndef bootstrap_oof_auroc(oof_syc, oof_wr, mask=None,\n                        n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    if mask is not None:\n        oof_syc = oof_syc[mask]\n        oof_wr = oof_wr[mask]\n    n = len(oof_syc)\n    if n < 4:\n        return None\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    aurocs = []\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc[idx], oof_wr[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            aurocs.append(roc_auc_score(labels, scores))\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    aurocs = np.array(aurocs)\n    full_scores = np.concatenate([oof_syc, oof_wr])\n    full_labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(n), np.zeros(n)])\n    point_auroc = float(roc_auc_score(full_labels, full_scores))\n    return {\"point_auroc\": point_auroc, \"bootstrap_mean\": float(np.mean(aurocs)),\n            \"std\": float(np.std(aurocs)),\n            \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 2.5)),\n            \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(aurocs, 97.5)),\n            \"n_pairs\": n, \"n_valid\": len(aurocs)}\n\ndef bootstrap_auroc_difference(oof_syc_d, oof_wr_d, oof_syc_r, oof_wr_r,\n                               n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP, seed=SEED):\n    n = len(oof_syc_d)\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    diffs = []\n    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)\n        scores_d = np.concatenate([oof_syc_d[idx], oof_wr_d[idx]])\n        scores_r = np.concatenate([oof_syc_r[idx], oof_wr_r[idx]])\n        labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(idx)), np.zeros(len(idx))])\n        try:\n            a_d = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_d)\n            a_r = roc_auc_score(labels, scores_r)\n            diffs.append(a_d - a_r)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    diffs = np.array(diffs)\n    return {\"mean_diff\": float(np.mean(diffs)),\n            \"ci_95_lower\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 2.5)),\n            \"ci_95_upper\": float(np.percentile(diffs, 97.5)),\n            \"p_greater_zero\": float(np.mean(diffs > 0)),\n            \"n_valid\": len(diffs)}\n\ndef random_direction_oof(syc, wr, domains_arr, n_random=200, seed=SEED,\n                         layer_lo=0, layer_hi=None):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n_pairs, n_layers, hidden = syc.shape\n    if layer_hi is None:\n        layer_hi = n_layers\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    folds = list(skf.split(np.arange(n_pairs), domains_arr))\n    random_aurocs = []\n    best_oof_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_oof_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n    best_overall = -1.0\n    for r in range(n_random):\n        d_rand = rng.randn(n_layers, hidden).astype(np.float32)\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(d_rand, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        d_rand = d_rand / np.maximum(norms, 1e-8)\n        fold_aurocs = []\n        tmp_syc = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n        tmp_wr = np.zeros(n_pairs)\n        for train_idx, test_idx in folds:\n            s_test = (syc[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n            w_test = (wr[test_idx] * d_rand).sum(axis=-1)\n            best_a = -1.0\n            best_l = layer_lo\n            for layer in range(layer_lo, layer_hi):\n                scores = np.concatenate([s_test[:, layer], w_test[:, layer]])\n                labels = np.concatenate([np.ones(len(test_idx)), np.zeros(len(test_idx))])\n                try:\n                    a = roc_auc_score(labels, scores)\n                    if a > best_a:\n                        best_a = a\n                        best_l = layer\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n            fold_aurocs.append(best_a)\n            tmp_syc[test_idx] = s_test[:, best_l]\n            tmp_wr[test_idx] = w_test[:, best_l]\n        mean_a = np.mean(fold_aurocs)\n        random_aurocs.append(mean_a)\n        if mean_a > best_overall:\n            best_overall = mean_a\n            best_oof_syc = tmp_syc.copy()\n            best_oof_wr = tmp_wr.copy()\n    return {\"mean_auroc\": float(np.mean(random_aurocs)), \"std\": float(np.std(random_aurocs)),\n            \"max_auroc\": float(np.max(random_aurocs)), \"n_random\": n_random}, best_oof_syc, best_oof_wr\n\ndef main():\n    t0 = time.time()\n    results = {}\n\n    # Qwen3-8B\n    print(\"Loading Qwen3-8B data (181 pairs)...\")\n    qwen_pairs = act_key=\"activations\", pos=1)\n    domains_q_arr = np.array(domains_q)\n    strengths_q_arr = np.array(strengths_q)\n    print(f\"  {syc_q.shape[0]} pairs, {syc_q.shape[1]} layers, {syc_q.shape[2]} dim\")\n\n    print(\"Running Qwen 5-fold CV...\")\n    q_mean, q_folds, q_oof_syc, q_oof_wr = cv_auroc(syc_q, wr_q, domains_q_arr, return_oof=True)\n    print(f\"  Mean CV AUROC = {q_mean:.4f}, folds = {[round(x,4) for x in q_folds]}\")\n    results[\"qwen_cv\"] = {\"point_estimate\": q_mean, \"fold_aurocs\": [round(x,6) for x in q_folds],\n                           \"fold_std\": float(np.std(q_folds)), \"n_pairs\": int(syc_q.shape[0])}\n\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen OOF AUROC (n=10000)...\")\n    q_oof_ci = bootstrap_oof_auroc(q_oof_syc, q_oof_wr, seed=SEED)\n    results[\"qwen_cv\"][\"oof_bootstrap\"] = q_oof_ci\n    print(f\"  OOF AUROC = {q_oof_ci['point_auroc']:.4f} [{q_oof_ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {q_oof_ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}]\")\n\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen per-strength AUROC...\")\n    results[\"qwen_per_strength\"] = {}\n    for strength in [\"weak\", \"medium\", \"strong\"]:\n        mask = strengths_q_arr == strength\n        ci = bootstrap_oof_auroc(q_oof_syc, q_oof_wr, mask=mask, seed=SEED)\n        if ci:\n            results[\"qwen_per_strength\"][strength] = ci\n            print(f\"  {strength}: AUROC = {ci['point_auroc']:.4f} [{ci['ci_95_lower']:.4f}, {ci['ci_95_upper']:.4f}] (n={ci['n_pairs']})\")\n\n    print(\"Bootstrapping Qwen full CV (n=10000)...\")\n    q_full_ci = bootstrap_cv_auroc(syc_q,
